# VaultForge Coding Tool List

Status: first-pass 1,000-tool inventory plus 825 alignment-expansion tools for later detail sweeps.
Count: 1,825 tool ideas; first 1,000 are balanced inventory; T1001-T1825 are uneven alignment-expansion tools.

Use: medium multi-agent tool builders should treat each line as a short build brief, not a final contract.
Merge rule: keep tools separate until a shared handler gives clearer scope, safer behavior, and better verification.
Safety rule: read-only first; preview-write before live-write; hard-gate secrets, broad filesystem access, paid APIs, network calls, destructive actions, and tunnel control.

Field legend:
- tool: proposed tool name.
- category: ordered group for build progression.
- rel: relevance tier for initial build priority.
- specialty: tool shape or behavior class.
- domain: area of work the tool belongs to.
- purpose: what the tool should do.
- build: key instruction for the builder thread.

Alignment expansion:
- T1001-T1825 intentionally rebalances toward real VaultForge, Codex/GPT, MCP, .4, Obsidian, Windows, cost, and handoff workflows.
- These categories are not equal-sized by design; uneven weight means higher observed workflow relevance.

Listing maintenance TODOs:
- Do not mark a first build batch until after merge, tagging, and detail sweeps.
- Run a merge-candidate pass for repeated scan/validate/summarize/route/export families that can share one handler while keeping fixed tool contracts.
- Run a near-duplicate pass across handoff, prompt, context, courier, artifact, and summary tools.
- Add priority tags without selecting build order yet: p0-foundation, p1-high-use, p2-specialized, p3-later.
- Add safety mode tags: read-only, preview-write, live-write-gated, external-gated, paid-gated, destructive-blocked.
- Add family tags such as family: registry, dot4-queue, mcp-proof, obsidian-plugin, handoff, cost-route, builder-packet, xp4l-event.
- Add implementation-shape tags such as path-list, json-report, markdown-report, status-summary, diff-summary, queue-report, handoff-pack.
- Check missing or thin areas before detailing: local MCP server building, ChatGPT app tool surfaces, API key and env handling, scheduler hooks, permission probes, report dashboards, telemetry, and tool health dashboards.

Detail sweep template:
- contract: inputs | outputs | reads | writes | blocked | proof
- Keep each detail entry compact; prefer one line of contract fields over paragraphs.
- Use exact allowed roots and artifact destinations where known.
- Keep live-write, paid, network, tunnel, destructive, and broad-filesystem behavior gated unless explicitly approved.
- Mark shared-handler candidates, but do not collapse names unless the resulting contracts stay clearer than separate tools.

Builder workflow artifacts:
- `tools/tool-list-builder-workflow.md` records the current integrity, merge-candidate, near-duplicate, tagging, and shared-handler sweep.
- `tools/tool-list-index.jsonl` is the machine-readable tagged index for all tools.
- `tools/tool-list-contracts.jsonl` is the generated compact contract layer for all tools.
- `tools/tool-runtime-build-status.md` records the current local runtime, verification, and live example batches.
- `tools/example-tool-result-build/` stores dry and live example outputs for built tool batches.

Build progression:
- 01. Core Registry & Contracts | tools T0001-T0050 | rel: foundation | domain: tool governance
- 02. Workspace Inventory & Discovery | tools T0051-T0100 | rel: foundation | domain: local repo discovery
- 03. Context Reading & Packing | tools T0101-T0150 | rel: foundation | domain: context control
- 04. Prompt & Handoff Ops | tools T0151-T0200 | rel: foundation | domain: agent handoff
- 05. Cost Token & Budget Control | tools T0201-T0250 | rel: foundation | domain: usage management
- 06. Run Artifacts & Logs | tools T0251-T0300 | rel: high | domain: run evidence
- 07. Requirements & Acceptance | tools T0301-T0350 | rel: high | domain: scope control
- 08. Build & Test Orchestration | tools T0351-T0400 | rel: high | domain: local verification
- 09. Code Review & Static Analysis | tools T0401-T0450 | rel: high | domain: code quality
- 10. Docs & Knowledge Base | tools T0451-T0500 | rel: high | domain: documentation
- 11. Git & Change Management | tools T0501-T0550 | rel: high | domain: change tracking
- 12. Dependency & Environment Health | tools T0551-T0600 | rel: high | domain: local environment
- 13. Security Secrets & Privacy | tools T0601-T0650 | rel: high | domain: safety controls
- 14. Data Validation & Schema | tools T0651-T0700 | rel: high | domain: structured data
- 15. Multi-Agent Workflow Routing | tools T0701-T0750 | rel: high | domain: parallel work coordination
- 16. Browser UI & API Smoke | tools T0751-T0800 | rel: medium | domain: runtime smoke testing
- 17. Refactor Patch & Migration Helpers | tools T0801-T0850 | rel: medium | domain: code change planning
- 18. Quality Gates & Release Readiness | tools T0851-T0900 | rel: high | domain: release control
- 19. VaultForge Obsidian Notes | tools T0901-T0950 | rel: specialized | domain: VaultForge notes
- 20. Tool Builder Meta Scaffolding | tools T0951-T1000 | rel: foundation | domain: tool construction

## 01. Core Registry & Contracts
